- **T76 (PR [#117](https://github.com/turkerurganci/Skinora/pull/117), ✓ PASS bağımsız validator 2026-05-17):** Blockchain Sidecar reconciliation job — 05 §3.3 günlük on-chain vs ledger karşılaştırma. Sidecar `TronGridClient.getAccountBalances` (`GET /v1/accounts/{addr}` TRX + TRC-20 contract→raw map) + `POST /api/wallet/balances` batch endpoint (max 100 addr, blockNumber tek seferlik shared, contract→symbol map config-driven `TRON_USDT_CONTRACT`/`TRON_USDC_CONTRACT`, test override-able). Backend: `IBlockchainSidecarClient.GetWalletBalancesAsync` typed result + `HttpBlockchainSidecarClient` impl (X-Internal-Key, 4xx→InvalidRequest, 5xx/timeout→Unavailable). `BlockchainTransactionType.SWEEP` enum eklendi (T73 K2 forward-deferred kapanışı — hot wallet expected hesabı için ledger satırı kategorisi; sweep dispatcher T-future PaymentReceivedEvent consumer). `AuditAction.RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH` + `AuditLogCategoryMap` SECURITY_EVENT eşlemesi (WALLET_ADDRESS_CHANGED + BOT_STATUS_CHANGED yanında — custody-integrity alarmı). `NotificationRealtimePayloads.AdminReconciliationMismatch` record + `INotificationRealtimePublisher.PublishAdminReconciliationMismatchAsync` + SignalR `Clients.All` broadcast (T69 AdminBotStatusChanged pattern). **`ReconciliationService` Skinora.API/Services/Reconciliation altında** (T63 cross-module pattern — Skinora.Realtime → Skinora.Transactions referans verdiği için Transactions modülünde olamaz, circular dependency); 3 scope reconciliation: (1) **DepositAddress** — aktif PaymentAddress'ler (MonitoringStatus != STOPPED), CONFIRMED inflow (BUYER_PAYMENT/WRONG_TOKEN_INCOMING/SPAM_TOKEN_INCOMING) − CONFIRMED outflow (SWEEP/REFUND family); (2) **HotWallet** (`reconciliation.hot_wallet_address` SystemSetting) — SWEEP inflow (ToAddress=hot) − HotWalletOutboundTypes outflow (FromAddress=hot, 6 refund/payout türü) − ColdWalletTransfer.Amount (FromAddress=hot); (3) **ColdWallet** (`reconciliation.cold_wallet_address` SystemSetting, opsiyonel) — ColdWalletTransfer.Amount (ToAddress=cold), MVP'de cold→external outflow yok. Tolerans 0 (05 §3.3 finansal invariant), stablecoin sabit USDT+USDC (08 §3.3 allowlist), TRX kapsam dışı (platform TRX ledger yok). In-flight koruma: yalnız `Status==CONFIRMED` beklenen toplama dahil → mid-finalization geçici mismatch raporlanmaz. Unconfigured hot/cold → skip + warn (production deploy sonrası devreye girer). - **T51 dersleri:** (1) **`AsNoTracking` aggregator + in-flight state**: `IReputationAggregator.RecomputeAsync` + `IUserCancelCooldownEvaluator.EvaluateAsync` AsNoTracking sorgu kullanıyor (T43 canonical state read pattern) — uncommitted change tracker'daki state'i göremez. Çözüm: `BeginTransactionAsync` scope'unda iki SaveChangesAsync — ilk save state flip + outbox publish, ikinci save user updates; tek transaction commit ile DB-level atomicity. Alternatif tracked-aware aggregator T43'te seçilmemişti, T51'de transaction wrap dayanıklı çözüm. T59 admin cancel + T58 dispute aynı pattern'i devralır. (2) **Pre-cancel state capture**: State machine OnEntry `CancelledAt` stamp'liyor, `Fire`'dan ÖNCE `previousStatus` + `itemWasOnPlatform = previousStatus == ITEM_ESCROWED` snapshot — T49 publisher'ın `previousStatus` parametre pattern'i ile aynı (T49 caller-side, T51 servis-içi). (3) **State machine `BuyerDecline` (TRADE_OFFER_SENT_TO_BUYER) permit ediyor ama T51 endpoint'i kapalı**: 02 §7 nedeniyle ödeme sonrası iki taraflı cancel yasak; `BuyerDecline` trigger T58 dispute (alıcı teslim itirazı) ve T59 admin manual akışları için reserved. (4) **Cooldown evaluator yalnız caller-side**: Aggregator zaten responsibility map ile non-responsible counter-party'yi filtreler (CANCELLED_SELLER + buyer caller → no-op); T59 admin cancel'da cooldown caller-side uygulanmaz (admin actor responsible değildir + 02 §13 CANCELLED_ADMIN responsibility map'inde dışlanmış). (5) **Türkçe bildirim metni inline literal** — T97 i18n full coverage'da resx'e taşınacak (T49 timeout reason metinleriyle birlikte). (6) **`ItemRefundToSellerRequestedEvent.Trigger` generalize**: T49 zaten merge edilmişti, generalize riskini taşıma yerine ayrı `TransactionCancelledItemReturnRequestedEvent` event'i alternatif olabilirdi ama her yeni cancel kaynağı (T59 admin) için yeni event çoğalır + Steam sidecar consumer iki event handle eder; tek event/tek consumer modeli yeniden adlandırılarak korundu. EnumTests count assertion 25 → 26 + ItemRefundTrigger explicit Theory + Fact eklendi (T49 dersinde "yeni enum eklerken count assertion manuel güncelleme" pattern'i tekrar). (7) **`paymentRefunded` her zaman false (T51 scope'u)**: 02 §7 nedeniyle T51 user-cancel post-payment kapalı; ödeme iadeli cancel forward-devir T49 timeout ✓ + T59 admin + T58 dispute. Yeni `Skinora.Shared/Enums/TimeoutPhase.cs` (`Accept`/`TradeOfferToSeller`/`Payment`/`Delivery` — 03 §4.1–§4.4 birebir) + 4 yeni outbox event: `TransactionTimedOutEvent` (notification fan-out trigger her 4 phase için, payload SellerId+BuyerId+ItemName+Phase), `ItemRefundToSellerRequestedEvent` (Steam sidecar T64–T68 forward, Payment+Delivery), `PaymentRefundToBuyerRequestedEvent` (Blockchain sidecar T73 forward, Delivery), `LatePaymentMonitorRequestedEvent` (Blockchain sidecar T75 forward, Payment). Yeni `Skinora.Transactions/Application/Timeouts/ITimeoutSideEffectPublisher` + `TimeoutSideEffectPublisher` — tek noktada phase → event mapping (caller `previousStatus` capture eder, publisher `IOutboxService.PublishAsync` change-tracker only ile uygun event(ler)i enlist eder); mapping: CREATED→Accept (sadece notification), TRADE_OFFER_SENT_TO_SELLER→TradeOfferToSeller (sadece notification), ITEM_ESCROWED→Payment (notification + item refund + late payment monitor), TRADE_OFFER_SENT_TO_BUYER→Delivery (notification + item refund + payment refund). Defensive logging: ITEM_ESCROWED'de BuyerId/RefundAddress null ise late payment monitor skip + log warning (06 §3.5'e göre olmamalı, schema regression bayraklaması); aynı koruma TRADE_OFFER_SENT_TO_BUYER → log error. `TimeoutExecutor` + `DeadlineScannerJob` `previousStatus` capture → state machine `Fire(Timeout)` → `_sideEffects.PublishAsync(transaction, previousStatus)` → tek `SaveChangesAsync` ile state flip + outbox satırları atomik commit (T48 `WarningDispatcher` deseni birebir, 09 §13.3 atomicity boundary). `TimeoutExecutor` sadece ITEM_ESCROWED için aktif (per-tx Hangfire job + scanner belt-and-suspenders), `DeadlineScannerJob` 4 phase'i de tarar. Yeni `Skinora.Notifications/Application/EventHandlers/TransactionTimedOutNotificationConsumer.cs` — `NotificationConsumerBase<TransactionTimedOutEvent>` türevi, ConsumerName `notifications.transaction-timed-out`, idempotent replay; her event için iki ayrı `NotificationRequest` (seller + buyer if registered) phase × role'a göre Türkçe `Reason` parametresi (03 §4.1–§4.4 metinleri verbatim) — mevcut `TRANSACTION_CANCELLED` template (`{ItemName} işlemi iptal edildi: {Reason}.`) tekrar kullanıldı, yeni enum/resx eklenmedi. Sidecar consumer'lar (T64–T68 Steam item refund, T73 Blockchain payment refund, T75 Blockchain late payment monitor) F4'te wire — T49 publisher tarafını production-ready yapar; T45 `TransactionCreatedEvent` + T46 `BuyerAcceptedEvent` events'leri (T62/T78–T80 forward consumer'lar) ile aynı pattern. DI: `TransactionsModule.cs` `ITimeoutSideEffectPublisher` Scoped kayıt. Migration yok, SystemSetting yok, yeni paket yok. **6/6 kabul kriteri** (4 tam ✓ + 2 kısmi: K#5 "doğru iade tetikleme" event publish ✓ + gerçek refund execution sidecar T64–T68/T73 forward; K#6 "gecikmeli ödeme izleme başlatma" event publish ✓ + gerçek monitor T75'te). Lokal Release 0W/0E + format verify (`dotnet format --verify-no-changes` exit=0) + tüm 11 test assembly **1237/1237 PASS** (T48: 1224 → T49: 1237, +13 net). Skinora.Transactions.Tests 391/391 → 403/403 (+12: 7 unit `TimeoutSideEffectPublisherTests` + 2 integration `TimeoutExecutorSideEffectsTests` + 3 integration `DeadlineScannerJobSideEffectsTests`), Skinora.Notifications.Tests 66/66 → 73/73 (+7: 4 unit `TransactionTimedOutNotificationConsumerTests` InlineData phase × role + 3 unit fan-out/skip/idempotency), Skinora.Shared.Tests EnumTests count assertion 24→25. - **T49 dersleri:** (1) **Outbox event vs inline call:** Timeout fire sonrası refund/notification side effect'leri inline değil outbox event olarak publish edildi — sebep: replayed Hangfire job'da consumer-idempotency garantisi (`IProcessedEventStore`), 09 §9.3 atomicity (state flip + event tek transaction'da commit), sidecar consumer'larının F4'te bağımsız implement edilmesi. T48 `WarningDispatcher` ve T45 `TransactionCreatedEvent` precedent'ları ile uyumlu. (2) **Phase-aware publisher tek nokta:** `ITimeoutSideEffectPublisher` mapping'i tek noktada tutuyor — TimeoutExecutor (per-tx Hangfire) ve DeadlineScannerJob (poller) aynı side effect'leri üretir. Caller'lar `previousStatus` capture sorumluluğu (Fire sonrası `transaction.Status` zaten CANCELLED_TIMEOUT). (3) **i18n forward-defer (T97):** Notification reason metinleri 03 §4.1–§4.4 verbatim Türkçe — 8 phase × role variants × 4 dil = 32 resx satırı T97'de eklenir; mevcut `TRANSACTION_CANCELLED_Body` `{Reason}` parametresi tekrar kullanıldı, yeni enum/resx şişmedi. (4) **Sidecar consumer F4 forward-deferred:** Event publisher tarafı production-ready, consumer kayıtları F4'te (T64–T68 Steam, T73/T75 Blockchain) — MediatR `Publish` zero handler'ı tolere eder, side effect'ler outbox row + log seviyesinde gözlemlenebilir. (5) **EnumTests count assertion:** Yeni enum eklerken `Skinora.Shared.Tests/Unit/EnumTests.cs` `AllEnums_ShouldExistInSharedNamespace` count assertion'ı manuel güncelleme gerekir (24→25); pattern T50+'de tekrar gelirse (yeni enum eklenirse) hatırlanmalı.

- **Checkpoints completed:** 19 (CP1-CP18, CP18 = 12 audit + GPT review + etki yansıtma + checkpoint)
